<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Copycat</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Shuffle your hand into your deck. Then, draw a card for each card in your opponent's hand.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Reprint of Copycat CES 127</em></p>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Gordie</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Look at the top 7 cards of your deck. You may reveal any number of Energy cards you find there and put them into your hand. Shuffle the other cards back into your deck.</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Raihan</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">You can only play this card if any of your Pokémon were Knocked Out during your opponent’s last turn.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Attach a basic Energy card from your discard pile to 1 of your Pokémon. If you do, search your deck for a card and put it in your hand. Then, shuffle your deck.</p>
</div>

</div>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Boost Shake</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Search your deck for a card that evolves from 1 of your Pokémon and put it onto that Pokémon to evolve it.   <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Snow Leaf Badge</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">If the Pokémon V this card is attached to has “Leafeon” or “Glaceon” in its name, it has no Retreat Cost and no Weakness.</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="row sqs-row"><div class="col sqs-col-2 span-2"><div class="sqs-block image-block sqs-block-image" data-block-type="5" data-sqsp-block="image-classic" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1625304146768_1793650"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Spirit Mask</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">If the Pokémon this card is attached to is in the Active Spot and is damaged by an attack from your opponent’s Pokémon <em>(even if it is Knocked Out)</em>, your opponent discards a card from their hand.</p>
</div>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Crystal Caves</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Once during each player’s turn, that player may heal 30 damage from each of their [M] Pokémon and [N] Pokémon.</p>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Stormy Mountains</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Once during each player’s turn, that player may search their deck for a Basic [L] or [N] Pokemon and put it to their Bench. Then, that player shuffles their deck.</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Treasure Energy</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">As long as this card is attached to a Pokémon, it provides [C]&nbsp;Energy.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">If you took this card as a face-down Prize card&nbsp;during your turn, before you put it into your hand, you may attach this card to 1 of your Pokémon.</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div></div></div>
